1448: function flowbar(){
1449:   const el=$("fb"); if(!el)return;
1450:   const k=key(), mine=FLOWS.filter(f=>f.steps.indexOf(k)>=0);
1451:   let h=mine.map(f=>{
1452:     const i=f.steps.indexOf(k);
1453:     return `<div class="fb"><span class="fb-t">${T(f.en,f.ko)}</span>`+
1454:       f.steps.map((st,j)=>`<span class="fb-s${j===i?" on":""}" data-go="${st}">${j+1}. ${esc(label(st))}</span>`)
1455:         .join('<span class="fb-a">\u2192</span>')+
1456:       `<span class="fb-n">${i+1} / ${f.steps.length}</span></div>`;
1457:   }).join("");
1458:   const nx=NEXT[k];
1459:   if(!mine.length&&nx){
1460:     h+=`<div class="fb"><span class="fb-t">${T("Next","다음")}</span>`+
1461:        `<span class="fb-s on">${esc(label(k))}</span><span class="fb-a">\u2192</span>`+
1462:        `<span class="fb-s" data-go="${nx}">${esc(label(nx))}</span></div>`;
1463:   }
1464:   if(!h){el.innerHTML="";el.style.display="none";return}
1465:   el.style.display=""; el.innerHTML=h; wireGo(el);
1466: }

1605: function goList(){
1606:   const host=$("gol"); if(!host)return;
1607:   const src=$("appin")||$("ad");
1608:   host.innerHTML=""; host.style.display="none";
1609:   if(!src)return;
1610:   const rows=goRows().slice();
1611:   const seen={}; rows.forEach(r=>seen[r.lab]=1);
1612:   [...src.querySelectorAll("[data-go]")].forEach(el=>{
1613:     const t=goLabel(el)||T("(no label)","(라벨 없음)");
1614:     if(seen[t])return; seen[t]=1;
1615:     rows.push({lab:t,dest:el.dataset.go});
1616:   });
1617:   host.style.display="";
1618:   let body;
1619:   if(!rows.length){
1620:     body='<div class="gonone">'+((typeof isDoc==="function")&&isDoc()
1621:       ?T("Explainer page — nothing to click.","설명 페이지입니다 — 클릭할 것이 없습니다.")
1622:       :T("No button leaves this screen. Filters and saves stay here.","이 화면에서 나가는 버튼이 없습니다. 필터·저장 등 같은 화면 동작만 있습니다."))+'</div>';
1623:   }else{
1624:     body=rows.map(r=>{
1625:       const lab=r.lab.length>34?r.lab.slice(0,34)+"\u2026":r.lab;
1626:       if(r.dest.charAt(0)==="@")
1627:         return '<div class="gorow x"><b>'+esc(lab)+'</b><span class="goar">\u2192</span><span class="gox">'+esc(r.dest.slice(1))+'</span></div>';
1628:       return '<div class="gorow"><b>'+esc(lab)+'</b><span class="goar">\u2192</span>'+
1629:              '<span class="gos" data-go="'+r.dest+'">'+esc(label(r.dest))+'</span>'+
1630:              '<span class="gok">'+r.dest+'</span></div>';
1631:     }).join("");
1632:   }
1633:   host.innerHTML='<div class="ic-h">'+T("Where clicks go","클릭하면 어디로 가나")+'</div>'+body;
1634:   wireGo(host);
1635: }

1687: /* 트리거 ↔ 선택지 배선 + 팝오버 */
1688: let DDOPEN=null;
1689: function ddClose(){
1690:   if(DDOPEN&&DDOPEN.parentNode)DDOPEN.parentNode.removeChild(DDOPEN);
1691:   DDOPEN=null;
1692:   document.querySelectorAll(".ddtrig.ddon").forEach(e=>e.classList.remove("ddon"));
1693:   const st=$("sbody"); if(st)st.classList.remove("ddup");
1694: }
1695: function ddVal(trig,v){
1696:   const pin=trig.querySelector(".pin");
1697:   const pfx=trig.dataset.ddpfx||"", dot=trig.dataset.dddot==="1";
1698:   trig.textContent=(dot?"⬤ ":"")+(pfx?pfx+": ":"")+v+" ▾";
1699:   if(pin)trig.appendChild(pin);
1700:   trig.classList.add("ddtrig");
1701: }
1702: function ddRender(set,cur){
1703:   const o=OPT[set]; if(!o)return "";
1704:   let h="";
1705:   if(o.sc){
1706:     h+='<div class="ddsc">'+o.sc.map(it=>{
1707:       const lab=T(it[0],it[1]), val=T(it[2]||it[0],it[3]||it[1]);
1708:       return '<span class="ddsb'+(val===cur?" on":"")+'" data-v="'+esc(val)+'">'+esc(lab)+'</span>';
1709:     }).join("")+'</div>';
1710:   }
1711:   o.g.forEach(gr=>{
1712:     if(gr[0])h+='<div class="ddh">'+esc(gr[0])+'</div>';
1713:     gr[1].forEach(it=>{
1714:       const lab=T(it[0],it[1]===undefined?it[0]:it[1]);
1715:       const tag=it[2]||"";
1716:       h+='<div class="ddo '+tag+'" data-v="'+esc(lab)+'">'+esc(lab)+
1717:          (tag==="new"?'<span class="ddt">'+T("new","신규")+'</span>':"")+
1718:          (tag==="no"?'<span class="ddt no">'+T("excluded","제외")+'</span>':"")+
1719:          (tag==="cex"?'<span class="ddt cex">'+T("CEX only","CEX 전용")+'</span>':"")+'</div>';
1720:     });
1721:   });
1722:   if(o.nt)h+='<div class="ddn">'+esc(T(o.nt[0],o.nt[1]))+'</div>';
1723:   return h;
1724: }
1725: function ddOpen(trig,set){
1726:   const was=trig.classList.contains("ddon");
1727:   ddClose();
1728:   if(was)return;
1729:   const d=document.createElement("div");
1730:   d.className="ddp";
1731:   const cur=(trig.textContent||"").replace(/\u25be/g,"").split(":").pop().trim();
1732:   d.innerHTML=ddRender(set,cur);
1733:   d.addEventListener("click",ev=>{
1734:     ev.stopPropagation();
1735:     const o=ev.target.closest(".ddo")||ev.target.closest(".ddsb"); if(!o)return;
1736:     ddVal(trig,o.dataset.v); ddClose();
1737:   });
1738:   trig.appendChild(d); trig.classList.add("ddon"); DDOPEN=d;
1739:   const st=$("sbody"); if(st)st.classList.add("ddup");
1740: }
1741: function wireDD(root){
1742:   if(!root)return;
1743:   const defs=(typeof DDS!=="undefined"&&DDS[key()])||[];
1744:   if(!defs.length)return;
1745:   const cands=[...root.querySelectorAll(".sel,.rsel,.chip,.dd-b,.inp")];
1746:   const hook=(el,d)=>{
1747:     if(el.dataset.ddset)return;
1748:     el.dataset.ddset=d[2];
1749:     if(d[3])el.dataset.ddpfx=d[3];
1750:     if((el.textContent||"").indexOf("⬤")>=0)el.dataset.dddot="1";
1751:     el.classList.add("ddtrig");
1752:     el.addEventListener("click",ev=>{ev.stopPropagation();ddOpen(el,d[2])});
1753:   };
1754:   defs.forEach(d=>{
1755:     if(String(d[0]).charAt(0)==="#"){
1756:       const el=cands[parseInt(d[0].slice(1),10)];
1757:       if(el)hook(el,d);
1758:       return;
1759:     }
1760:     const m=T(d[0],d[1]);
1761:     cands.filter(e=>(e.textContent||"").indexOf(m)>=0).forEach(e=>hook(e,d));
1762:   });
1763: }
1764: document.addEventListener("click",ddClose);
1765: /* 화면 안 토글 — data-sub 로 같은 화면의 다른 상태를 그린다 */
1766: function wireSub(root){
1767:   if(!root)return;
1768:   root.querySelectorAll("[data-sub]").forEach(el=>{
1769:     el.classList.add("clk");
1770:     el.addEventListener("click",ev=>{
1771:       if(ev.target.closest(".pin"))return;
1772:       ev.stopPropagation(); S.sub=el.dataset.sub; S.act=null; stage();
1773:     });
1774:   });
1775: }
1776: /* 축약된 값 — 누르면 전체가 보인다 */
1777: function wireFull(root){
1778:   if(!root)return;
1779:   root.querySelectorAll("[data-full]").forEach(el=>{
1780:     if(el.dataset.fullWired)return;
1781:     el.dataset.fullWired="1";
1782:     el.dataset.fullShort=(el.textContent||"").trim();
1783:     el.classList.add("fullv");
1784:     el.title=T("Click to show in full","누르면 전체가 보입니다");
1785:     el.addEventListener("click",ev=>{
1786:       if(ev.target.closest(".pin"))return;
1787:       ev.stopPropagation();
1788:       const on=el.classList.toggle("open");
1789:       const pin=el.querySelector(".pin");
1790:       el.textContent=on?el.dataset.full:el.dataset.fullShort;
1791:       if(pin)el.appendChild(pin);
1792:     });
1793:   });
1794: }
1795: /* 드래그로 순서 바꾸기 */
1796: function ddNumber(list){
1797:   [...list.children].forEach((r,i)=>{const o=r.querySelector(".ord");if(o)o.textContent=i+1;});
1798: }
1799: function wireDnD(root){
1800:   if(!root)return;
1801:   root.querySelectorAll("[data-dnd]").forEach(list=>{
1802:     let src=null;
1803:     [...list.children].forEach(row=>{
1804:       row.setAttribute("draggable","true");
1805:       row.classList.add("drg");
1806:       row.addEventListener("dragstart",e=>{src=row;row.classList.add("dragging");
1807:         if(e.dataTransfer)e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed="move";});
1808:       row.addEventListener("dragend",()=>{row.classList.remove("dragging");src=null;ddNumber(list);});
1809:       row.addEventListener("dragover",e=>{
1810:         e.preventDefault(); if(!src||src===row)return;
1811:         const r=row.getBoundingClientRect();
1812:         const after=r.height?((e.clientY-r.top)/r.height>0.5):false;
1813:         list.insertBefore(src,after?row.nextSibling:row);
1814:       });
1815:     });
1816:     ddNumber(list);
1817:   });
1818: }
1819: function applyNotes(){
1820:   const a=$("sbody");
1821:   a.querySelectorAll(".pin").forEach(e=>e.remove());
1822:   a.querySelectorAll(".noted").forEach(e=>e.classList.remove("noted","act","has-pin"));
1823:   NOTES=[...a.querySelectorAll("[data-kind]")].map((el,i)=>({el,n:i+1,kind:el.dataset.kind,en:el.dataset.en,ko:el.dataset.ko}));
1824:   if(S.notes) NOTES.forEach(o=>{
1825:     if(getComputedStyle(o.el).position==="static") o.el.classList.add("has-pin");
1826:     o.el.classList.add("noted");
1827:     const b=document.createElement("button");
1828:     b.className="pin k-"+o.kind; b.textContent=o.n; b.type="button";
1829:     b.setAttribute("aria-label",T("Change","변경점")+" "+o.n);
1830:     b.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();focus(o.n)};
1831:     o.el.appendChild(b);
1832:   });
1833:   applyGo($("appin"));
1834:   ddClose();
1835:   wireDD($("appin"));
1836:   wireSub($("appin"));
1837:   wireFull($("appin"));
1838:   wireDnD($("appin"));
1839:   wireGo(a);
1840:   goList();
1841:   panel(); pbar(); bind(); flowbar();
1842: }
1843: function panel(){
1844:   const kn=KN();
1845:   $("np").innerHTML=`<div class="np-h"><b>${T("What changes here","이 상태에서 바뀌는 것")}</b><span class="bdg gy">${NOTES.length}</span></div>
1846:    <div class="np-lg"><span><i style="background:var(--k-new)"></i>${kn.new}</span><span><i style="background:var(--k-chg)"></i>${kn.chg}</span><span><i style="background:var(--k-lang)"></i>${kn.lang}</span><span><i style="background:var(--k-lang)"></i>${kn.lang}</span><span><i style="background:var(--k-pol)"></i>${kn.pol}</span><span><i style="background:var(--k-ask)"></i>${kn.ask}</span></div>
1847:    <div class="np-l">${NOTES.length?NOTES.map(o=>`<div class="np-i${S.act===o.n?" act":""}" data-n="${o.n}"><span class="pin st k-${o.kind}">${o.n}</span><div class="tx"><b class="kn">[${kn[o.kind]}]</b> ${esc(T(o.en,o.ko))}</div></div>`).join(""):`<div class="np-e">${T("Nothing changes in this state.","이 상태에는 변경점이 없습니다.")}</div>`}</div>`;
1848: }
1849: function focus(n){
1850:   S.act=S.act===n?null:n;
1851:   document.querySelectorAll(".noted").forEach(e=>e.classList.remove("act"));
1852:   const o=NOTES.find(x=>x.n===S.act); if(o)o.el.classList.add("act");
1853:   panel();
1854:   const t=document.querySelector('.np-i[data-n="'+n+'"]');
1855:   if(t&&S.act)t.scrollIntoView({behavior:"smooth",block:"nearest"});
1856: }
1857: function bind(){
1858:   const ck=$("ck"); if(ck)ck.onchange=e=>{S.notes=e.target.checked;applyNotes()};
1859:   $("pbar").querySelectorAll("[data-v]").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{S.view=b.dataset.v;stage()});
1860:   $("pbar").querySelectorAll("[data-l]").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{S.lang=b.dataset.l;S.act=null;all()});
1861:   const j=$("jump"); if(j)j.onclick=()=>$("np").scrollIntoView({behavior:"smooth",block:"start"});
1862:   $("np").querySelectorAll(".np-i").forEach(d=>d.onclick=()=>focus(+d.dataset.n));
1863: }

1866: $("g2").onclick=e=>{const b=e.target.closest("button");if(!b)return;S.si=+b.dataset.i;S.act=null;S.sub=null;g2();stage()};
